Method: 首先，针对由高程图给定的环境进行基于坡度的地形可通行性分析，将地图降维为二维可通行图，将不可通行区域视为障碍物；其次，设计模糊推理系统（FISs）用于生成多机器人系统搬运物体到目标位置的速度；最后，使用遗传算法在包括局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物及杂乱环境等多种场景下对FIS进行离线训练优化。

Result: 训练好的FIS模型被应用于转换后的可通行图对应的测试环境，并通过多种场景进行了验证，证明了所提方法在多机器人协作搬运任务中的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于遗传模糊系统的分散式方法能够有效解决非结构化环境下多机器人协作搬运问题，在降低总路径长度的同时具备良好的障碍物规避能力和场景泛化性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [169] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，通过当前到未来的空间地图想象（current-to-future spatial map imagination）来解决无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）中的场景理解与未来空间推理问题。框架先学习结构化当前地图表示，并通过联合监督编码场景结构与运动意图，再在结构化因果注意力下将当前空间知识传播至未来地图推理，最后聚合当前与未来表示预测下一个3D航点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型方法大多直接将视觉-语言输入映射到动作，缺乏显式的场景锚定（scene grounding）和面向未来的空间推理能力，难以在复杂户外环境中准确执行3D运动。

Method: 1) 从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示；2) 通过当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测联合监督该表示；3) 在结构化因果注意力下将当前空间知识传播至未来地图推理；4) 聚合当前与未来表示预测下一个3D航点；5) 引入跨空间规划一致性损失，使预测的地图空间轨迹与基于真值航点位移的专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上取得了强性能，消融实验验证了框架各个组件的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight通过显式地想象当前与未来的空间地图结构，并引入跨空间一致性约束，显著提升了UAV-VLN场景中智能体的空间感知与未来规划能力。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [170] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一个基于自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）的连续时间视觉惯性导航系统，相比均匀样条基线和离散时间方法在多种运动条件下取得相当或更低的轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离散时间视觉惯性导航系统（如IMU预积分方法）在任意时间戳评估残差或需要运动相关的时间分辨率时灵活性不足；而连续时间样条方法虽然缓解了这一问题，但均匀分布的节点在快速动态区域表示不足、在静态区间又过度参数化。因此需要一种能自适应分配节点、支持局部细化且保持观测一致性的连续时间框架。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS由三个核心模块组成：(1) 自适应节点放置（AKP）根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；(2) 多分辨率样条（MRS）在切空间中添加有界的局部细化能力；(3) 3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）通过将变换后的特征与校准观测射线对齐，在3D测量空间中提供方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明，ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动和传感条件下均达到与对比基线相当或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应的连续时间轨迹表示（结合自适应节点、多分辨率细化与3D测量空间残差）能够在不牺牲精度的情况下，灵活应对动态变化场景，为VINS提供了优于均匀样条和传统离散方法的选择。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [171] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个面向自动驾驶的统一规划框架，通过结构化动作空间协调机制将语义推理（VLA）和预测动力学（WAM）两条路径对齐，并采用异步整流流推理策略缩短推理延迟，在NAVSIM v1和v2上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划需要同时满足语义约束和预测性动力学，但现有端到端方法（VLA和WAM）各自只擅长其中一个方面。朴素地通过联合token级注意力组合两者会导致注意力分配失配问题——语义捷径主导共享注意力空间而抑制预测动力学。

Method: 受神经科学中"复杂行为源于功能特化系统间的协调"这一发现的启发，BrainWAM将语义推理和预测世界建模转化为两条特化的动作导向通路，并在紧凑动作表示层面进行对齐。同时提出异步整流流推理策略，通过解耦视频和动作去噪来缩短推理延迟同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均达到SOTA性能，并持续优于仅使用VLA或仅使用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM验证了结构化动作空间协调是统一语义先验和预测动力学的有效范式，为自动驾驶系统提供了实用且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [172] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的物理仿真VLN平台和基准，支持多种人形机器人形态（10-12自由度、1.17m-1.80m高度），通过分层控制栈（强化学习步态策略+PD/MPC路径跟踪器）实现腿部运动约束下的导航。包含933个碰撞感知参考episode，利用双生成器-评审器+改写器多智能体流水线生成指令。四模型四形态评测中，JanusVLN达到最高成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38；20-episode仿真到真实场景pilot实验中，DualVLN与Unitree G1的导航误差高度相关（r=0.935）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准无法解决人形机器人的关键挑战：双足运动带来的物理约束（轮式智能体不存在）、各平台人形形态差异、以及运动诱发相机动态导致的自我中心观测畸变。需要一个新的物理仿真基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建物理仿真平台，支持可扩展的人形配置（Unitree G1/H1、Internal-A/B），采用层次化控制栈（RL步态策略+可互换的PD或MPC路径跟踪器）。2) 场景来自艺术家设计及3D高斯泼溅重建，筛选可导航面积超100平方米。3) 采用双生成器-评审器+改写器多智能体流水线（含人类验证）生成指令，产出933个碰撞感知参考episode，每个配1条细粒度指令和3条粗粒度风格变体。4) 在四模型四形态上评测，并进行20-episode仿真到真实pilot验证。

Result: JanusVLN在四模型四形态评测中取得最优性能（成功率43.55%，nDTW 48.38）。仿真到真实的pilot实验中（DualVLN+Unitree G1），导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度nDTW为0.782（±0.188），展示了物理执行下VLN模型、控制器与人形形态的交互效应。

Conclusion: HumanoidVLN填补了人形机器人VLN物理仿真基准的空白，不仅提供了多样化形态与物理约束下的评测能力，还通过强仿真-真实相关性验证了平台的实用价值。代码、基准和数据将在录用后开源发布。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [173] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于有源LED标记的AUV间相对位姿估计框架，通过红色中心LED与三颗蓝色外围LED构成的标记模块，结合概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），在复杂水下环境中实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计。水槽实验与闭环领航-跟随实验验证了其精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人（AUV）之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探测与多机器人协调至关重要，但水下环境中存在浑浊、光照变化、反射及特征间歇遮挡等严重光学退化问题，使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战。

Method: 设计紧凑的LED标记模块（一个红色中心LED加三个蓝色外围LED）集成于领航AUV上，提供独特视觉特征；在此基础上开发概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），融合SE(3)上的Lie群位姿传播、概率标记关联与可见性自适应测量融合，动态适应标记可见性变化，在部分观测与可见性切换期间保持几何一致性与时间稳定性。

Result: 在带运动捕捉真值的水槽实验中，AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环领航-跟随实验进一步验证了其在协同水下机器人中对实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于有源LED标记与概率切换PnP估计的方法能够有效克服水下光学退化问题，为AUV协同作业提供精确且稳定的相对位姿估计，具备实际部署与实时应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [174] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Temporal GRPO方法，通过将轨迹按任务阶段分段并计算阶段级优势来解决VLA策略后训练中的轨迹级信用分配问题，在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long上显著提升任务成功率与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO在VLA后训练中为整条轨迹分配单一优势，当一条轨迹前期完成多个有效阶段但后续失败时，前期的正确动作会被错误惩罚。这种"轨迹级信用别名"问题导致信用分配不准确，限制了策略学习效率。

Method: Temporal GRPO通过三个步骤解决该问题：1) 构建可检测的任务阶段；2) 将每条轨迹与特定阶段对应的动作区间对齐；3) 仅比较进入同一阶段的rollout轨迹，计算阶段级优势并将其应用于对应动作区间，在单次策略更新中完成。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和样本效率，在各任务horizon上均有一致的收益；在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验证明该方法保留了共享的先决阶段能力，并将改进集中在轨迹结果首次出现分歧的阶段。

Conclusion: 通过阶段级信用分配替代轨迹级信用分配，Temporal GRPO有效缓解了轨迹级信用别名问题，为基于结果的VLA策略后训练提供了更精确且可扩展的优化信号。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [175] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了H2R-Bench基准，用于评估跨具身（从人类到机器人）的操控视频生成模型的性能，通过将人类第一视角操控视频转换为指定机器人具身下的操控视频，并从五个维度系统评估生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要大规模操控数据，但收集真实机器人示范数据成本高且难以扩展；虽然人类第一视角操控视频资源丰富，但由于人类手与机器人末端执行器之间的具身差异，将这些经验跨具身迁移到机器人面临巨大挑战。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准：(1)每个实例包含人类示范视频、目标具身约束以及源接地标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能接触、物体响应）；(2)从目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和视频质量五个维度评估生成的机器人操控视频；(3)在六个操控家族和两种机器人具身上基准测试11个SOTA视频生成模型。

Result: 评估表明当前的视频世界模型在人类到机器人的操控迁移中仍然存在明显局限：即使是领先模型也经常在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面失败，揭示了现有模型在跨具身操控视频生成上的不足。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench提供了一个系统的诊断框架，用于评估视频世界模型是否能够弥合人类与机器人之间的具身差距，并将人类操控观察转化为可供机器人训练使用的资源，为该领域的后续研究提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [176] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的训练与评估模拟器。SRF将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升为3D辐射场，同时编码几何、外观与逐类语义身份，能够从真实场景的RGB采集重建，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，统一了真实感与语义真实性的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间推理具身智能体训练环境存在两难困境：合成模拟器提供真实语义标注但缺乏视觉真实感；基于真实场景重建的模拟器外观真实但默认缺少语义标注。需要一个同时满足几何保真和语义可查询的多样化环境。

Method: 利用语义辐射场（SRF）作为模拟器：将预训练视觉模型生成的2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场中，实现几何、外观与语义的联合编码。从带位姿的RGB图像重建真实场景，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并可接入物理引擎。

Result: 文章以一个果园苹果采摘任务为例展示SRF驱动的模拟器：辐射场向物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占用查询，从而实现多样化真实世界环境的生成，用于训练和评估空间推理模型。

Conclusion: SRF作为模拟器统一了真实感与语义真实性，为空间推理智能体提供了可扩展的真实世界仿真环境生成方案，有效弥合了合成模拟器与真实重建模拟器之间的差距。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [177] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM，一种稀疏事件结构化的分层世界模型，通过学习原始潜在轨迹中的稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理者和原始步工作者，在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上达到98.7%的成功率，比扁平GAS DreamerV3基线高22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术机器人操作任务中，任务奖励稀疏且有意义的交互变化发生在不规则间隔处。现有世界模型代理通常在原始步分辨率下进行想象，隐式地忽略了可变时长的任务进度；手动指定阶段虽然能提供中间结构，但其任务特定边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM包含三个核心组件：1) 从原始潜在轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理者的目标更新；2) 每个选定的潜在目标条件化原始步工作者的动作直到下一次更新；3) 学习到的事件证据形成可变时长分段，用于事件转换模型(ETM)，预测下一个边界随机状态、分段时长和累积分段奖励。通过链接这些事件级预测，在原始想象视界之外提供可变时长的延续用于管理者学习，同时工作者保持原始步的演员-评论家学习。

Result: 在SurRoL-based PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM达到98.7%的成功率，比扁平GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过稀疏事件结构化的分层世界模型有效解决了长时程手术操作中奖励稀疏和交互间隔不规则的问题，事件级与原始步级的协同学习显著提升了任务成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [178] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂FAM-DQ，通过在中央框架两端安装四旋翼推进模块并以齿轮驱动舵机调节指向，实现了6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制，具备全向操作能力与高扭矩输出，最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比为2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在位置-姿态强耦合问题，而全驱动平台设计往往面临结构复杂、有效载荷有限和扭矩输出不足等挑战，难以满足空中实体交互中对大力矩和精确末端控制的需求。

Method: 提出FAM-DQ结构：将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装在中央框架两端，利用齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，实现6自由度解耦控制与全向操作。通过轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等实验验证设计。

Result: FAM-DQ在总质量仅0.447 kg的情况下实现最大扭矩1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg，并通过多种实验验证了系统的轨迹跟踪、姿态控制与实体交互能力。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过双四旋翼与被动关节的创新设计，在保持结构简洁的同时实现了全驱动6自由度控制与高扭矩输出，为空中实体交互任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [179] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对制造复杂软体气动执行器的多种工艺路线（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉基/液基增材制造以及FDM）进行了系统评估，通过工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进，区分了工艺固有局限与可纠正缺陷。结果表明FDM是最具适应性的路线，且气密性不仅取决于名义壁厚还取决于挤出路径架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器需要在几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性之间同时取得平衡，而现有制造工艺缺乏系统性评估以指导工艺选择，因此需要一项以制造为核心的评估研究来建立工艺选择的设计准则。

Method: 采用四阶段评估框架：工艺筛选（process screening）、基线制造（baseline fabrication）、失效分析（failure analysis）和工艺改进（process improvement），系统比较热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉基与液基增材制造及FDM等工艺路线。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何保形性，浇铸受限于模具可达性与粘结界面，粉基方法受限于密闭通道内残留粉末，数字光处理受限于材料属性与后处理要求；FDM因可通过工艺优化逐步消除主要缺陷而成为最灵活的路线。研究发现气密性同时取决于壁厚和挤出路径架构，且无支撑几何对内部后处理受限的场景至关重要。

Conclusion: 建立了以制造为导向的设计方法，工艺选择应以执行器结构与制造约束之间的兼容性为依据，为开发复杂、柔性且气密的软体气动执行器提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [180] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级、模块化的主动碰撞避免框架，在遥操作系统的末端执行器速度指令层面运作。该框架通过预处理点云，基于飞行碰撞时间（TTC）预测潜在碰撞并集成过冲保护，随后利用罗德里格斯旋转公式偏转相对速度向量，仅改变方向而保持大小不变，从而缓解传统人工势场（APF）方法的死锁问题。仿真和物理实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作系统虽然使机器人具备类人的灵巧操作能力，但操作者在网络延迟或注意力受限的情况下可能无法及时有效地避开突然出现的障碍物，造成安全风险。现有方法（如传统APF）存在死锁问题，需要提出一种更可靠的主动碰撞避免方案。

Method: 该框架直接作用于末端执行器的速度指令层，包含两个核心模块：(1) 碰撞预测模块：预处理点云后，基于飞行碰撞时间（TTC）预测潜在碰撞，并集成过冲保护，同时补偿复杂遥操作管线中引入的点云处理延迟；(2) 速度偏转模块：利用罗德里格斯旋转公式对相对速度向量进行旋转偏转，仅改变速度方向而保持大小不变。轻量化设计支持遥操作所需的高频率控制。

Result: 在多样化的仿真场景中，所提方法相比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免率；在物理机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其碰撞避免的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于TTC预测与罗德里格斯旋转的框架，有效解决了遥操作中突发障碍物的主动避碰问题，在保持速度大小的同时改变方向，避免了传统APF的死锁缺陷，并在仿真和物理实验中均展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [181] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套策略学习框架，通过让操作者仅控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调控手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹，从而降低灵巧操作演示采集的难度，实现可靠高效的真实世界灵巧操纵学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖手臂运动与精确、接触丰富的手指行为的协调，但收集一致、完整的任务级演示非常困难，成为灵巧操作学习的瓶颈。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务需要操作者全程协调手臂与手指行为，直接学习这些行为受限于演示数据的难以获取。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套策略框架：内层手部策略从最新的本体感受历史中自适应调节手指运动，视觉-语言选择器按任务阶段激活合适技能；操作者控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调控活动的手部技能。所采集的演示用于训练一个独立的外层视觉运动策略，部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂和手部。此外，使用手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间的手臂指令。

Result: 在真实世界灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提升了演示的可靠性和效率，实证评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过解耦手臂控制与手部技能选择，显著降低了灵巧操作演示采集的负担，为灵巧操作任务的自主策略学习提供了可行的途径。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [182] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA是一种面向自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的失败感知自进化框架。当GRPO采样的轨迹组整体质量差、多样性不足时，该框架利用冻结的轮次起始教师模型进行自蒸馏，将无法解决的失败转化为下一轮策略的特权监督。教师与学生参数规模相同，但教师能观察到隐藏的未来轨迹，且监督仅作用于答案token，GRPO仍保持激活。在6,019个nuScenes样本上，FIRE-VLA将G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，持久失败率从13.03%降至11.20%，且改进主要来自罕见严重rollout的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖组内奖励差异作为相对信号，当所有采样轨迹都较差时，该相对信号只能对失败进行排序，无法识别超出失败区域的行为模式，导致策略无法在这些情况下有效改进。现有方法未能解决这种"未解决的失败"（unresolved failures）问题。

Method: FIRE-VLA在低奖励、低多样性的组中触发从冻结的轮次起始模型副本的自蒸馏。教师与学生参数规模相同，但仅教师能观察隐藏的未来轨迹（特权信息）。监督遵循学生生成的前缀且仅作用于答案token，同时GRPO对所有组保持激活。更新的策略作为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化，无需更大规模的外部教师。

Result: 在6,019个样本、150个nuScenes场景上，FIRE-VLA保持了可比的单样本规划性能，将G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，评估持久失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。平均误差的降低主要源于罕见的严重失败轨迹的减少，而非普通轨迹的均匀改进。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过将GRPO无法解决的失败转化为特权监督信号，实现了策略的自我演化，显著降低了严重轨迹误差和持久失败率，同时保持了与基线相当的计算成本（相同的rollout和策略更新次数），无需外部更大教师模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [183] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"作动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）理念的一体化设计-建模-感知框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的前提下实现实时缆绳张力测量；并提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架以处理空间缆绳布线带来的建模复杂性，结合近端多轴力/力矩传感器实现稳定的形状与力感知。实验在单段和双段连续体手术机器人上验证了实时末端位姿估计及接触力与位置感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绞盘驱动的连续体手术机器人在紧凑绞盘组件内难以获取缆绳张力信息，导致形状感知和力感知成为长期制约其发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一个三层框架：(1) 设计层：在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，使缆绳反作用力下产生微变形，从而实现不占用紧凑绞盘空间的实时缆绳张力测量；(2) 建模层：提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架，捕获短梁段的剪切效应和多种缆绳协同交互，实现实时性能；(3) 感知层：结合近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，实现稳定的形状和力感知。先后开发了单段和双段的绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人。

Result: 在单段和双段连续体机器人上的实验验证了该框架的有效性，实现了实时末端位姿估计以及接触力和接触位置的感知。该框架在不牺牲紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现了缆绳张力反馈，使集成感知在绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人上成为可能。

Conclusion: 通过作动-感知协同设计理念，该框架为绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人提供了一种可行的集成感知解决方案，解决了形状和力传感的关键瓶颈，在不影响紧凑绞盘结构的前提下实现了实时缆绳张力反馈与稳定的形状/力感知。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [184] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Deliberate Practice (DP)的主动技能学习算法，在有限练习预算下为顺序任务计算最优的技能练习分配方案，通过双线性规划实现预算最优分配，并在模拟和真实世界的长时程操作任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习顺序任务中的技能时，练习预算（时间）是有限的，需要合理的分配策略来最大化学习收益。然而，在大型练习预算下，需要考虑组合爆炸的技能规划方案，这使得计算最优分配极具挑战性。

Method: DP算法同时估计技能掌握所需时间以及技能所解锁的任务规划累积回报，并将预算最优分配问题建模为一个双线性规划，可直接使用现成的求解器精确求解。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时程操作任务实验中，该方法使得机器人能够在有限的练习时间内最优地获取有用策略，并改善长时程规划效果。

Conclusion: DP算法为有限练习预算下的主动技能学习提供了可证明的预算最优分配方案，为机器人在资源受限条件下的自主技能获取提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [185] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的视觉读出机制，从动作监督中学习注意力，生成随任务进度演化的感兴趣区域（ROI），用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤，显著提升视觉运动学习的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ROI接口要么依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别、可操作性标注），要么依赖基于轨迹的固定裁剪（如以末端执行器为中心），这些方法在时间事件选择、代理点和裁剪尺度上存在固定偏差。当控制所需的视觉证据远离末端执行器或随任务进度连续变化时，此类裁剪会错位，限制数据效率。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询（query）来聚合视觉证据，仅依赖动作监督即可生成任务进度感知的ROI。该学习到的ROI作为空间接口，同时用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强以及点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，Seeker在数据效率和鲁棒性上均优于无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪等基线。真实机器人实验中，域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，在光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从动作中学习注意力而非依赖外部标签或固定启发式裁剪，Seeker提供了一种无需额外标签的高效空间先验接口，能够适应任务动态变化并显著改善视觉运动学习的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [186] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactGuard是一种预接触执行监控器，用于分块视觉运动策略。它在机器人实际接触物体前，利用潜在空间世界模型预测策略动作块的结果，并通过轻量级故障探针判断预测的潜在表示是否预示失败，从而在接触发生前中止执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，失败往往在机器人已经接触物体之后才被检测到。在腕部相机设置下这个问题尤为严重：虽然近距视觉有助于观察接触，但糟糕的接近动作可能已经推、漏、滑或扰动物体，而传统检测器反应不及。

Method: (1) 学习一个潜在世界模型，从无标注机器人轨迹中训练，预测在给定动作下紧凑的多视角视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；(2) 训练一个轻量级故障探针，利用小规模标注的预接触片段；(3) 部署时在即将接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下滚动前向模型，并验证预测的后接触潜在表示。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard比直接基线（分块执行预测）和损坏动作消融基线（引入人为噪声的动作）更准确地预测失败，并能作为预接触中止信号实时迁移到实体机器人，无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard证明了在接触发生之前（而非之后）预测接触丰富操作失败是可行的，通过在潜在视觉空间中进行短时滚动预测配合轻量级故障探针，能够在不需要像素级预测或策略修改的情况下实现有效的中止机制。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [187] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社交机器人在领域增量持续学习场景中学习跨多个室内环境的适当行为，通过显式分离环境知识和社会智能体知识，并结合基于回放的重演机制缓解灾难性遗忘，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要在多样化的环境中运行，相似的环境布置可能对应不同的社会适当行为（如拥挤家庭中可交谈但会议室中会打扰）。这些规范和情境无法预先全部预见，因此机器人需要持续学习能力以从顺序经验中适应，同时保留已有知识。现有研究虽已涉及社交适当行为生成的持续学习，但尚未处理领域增量设置——即机器人逐步遇到不同室内场景，其中环境线索（空间开放或家具杂乱）和社会线索（人或其他智能体的位置）共同决定行为适当性。

Method: 提出显式解耦双分支（Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch, EDD）框架。EDD显式地分离环境相关知识和社交智能体相关知识，并使用基于回放的重演机制来缓解忘记旧知识的问题，同时在多个室内领域上学习机器人行为（如清洁、服务、开启对话）的适当性。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个最先进基线上表现出更优的性能；消融研究进一步评估了不同的解耦策略以及模型对领域顺序的敏感性。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 显式解耦环境与社会智能体知识并辅以重演机制，是解决社交机器人领域增量持续学习中行为适当性学习问题的有效途径，该框架在基准上展现出了优越性。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [188] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）π0.5的残差流进行机制可解释性探测，发现任务进度（轨迹剩余归一化时间）可以从激活中线性读出。该信号在预训练的PaliGemma骨干中就存在（无需机器人数据），线性探针可泛化到未见任务，并可作为无需标签的分布外（OOD）检测器来检测任务停滞，性能与现有最先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型正快速走向部署，成为通用操作策略，但目前缺乏理解其内部表征的基础工具，也缺乏运行时监控手段。作者希望借助机制可解释性的思路，揭示这些模型中语义量是如何被表征的，并为部署监控提供轻量级方案。

Method: 在π0.5的残差流上训练线性探针来读取"任务进度"（归一化的轨迹剩余时间）。作者验证了该信号在预训练PaliGemma骨干（未经过机器人数据训练）中已存在；在多提示数据上探针可泛化到未见任务并随语言反事实变化；同时测试了探针驱动的策略操控（steering）是否可行，并利用该探针构建无标签OOD检测器以检测任务停滞。

Result: 任务进度可从VLA激活中线性读出；信号源自预训练PaliGemma骨干；线性探针泛化到未见任务并在多提示下随语言反事实变化，但无法实现有效的策略steering；该探针作为无标签OOD检测器在检测任务停滞上与最先进方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部存在丰富、可线性读取的语义量（如任务进度）表征，读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [189] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于浏览器的海洋滑翔机规划器数字试验场，无需安装即可将选定区域转化为可复现的四维实验环境。系统通过统一的"计划到观测"契约将手动航线、内置算法和导入的规划器输出整合，在54次任务实验中评估了5种经典规划器，并实现了GliderFlight 1.2.0的浏览器原生移植，建立了面向部署前实验的高保真多层级模拟路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复现场评估面临稀缺的运载工具、操作人员、部署与回收资源，以及无法为竞争算法重置的海洋条件，导致算法间难以进行公平、可复现的对比评估。

Method: 构建一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字试验场，涵盖从区域选择、任务范围水深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标、任务分解、路径规划、洋流平流执行、观测生成到评分打分全流程。核心贡献是制定统一的计划到观测契约，统一运载工具、传感和评估者假设；通过OSSE实验评估5种经典规划器在两次情景、三个确定性种子和60小时地平线下的表现；并通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的源锁定原生到浏览器移植。

Result: 全部54次任务均成功完成并恢复且无硬性违规；规划器排名和潜水策略效应揭示了运行-科学权衡；真实公开部署提供了现场参考审计以界定当前运动学边界；GliderFlight在浏览器中实现了原生对等性能，验证了高保真多层级模拟的可行性。

Conclusion: 该系统提供了科学可追溯且组件合格的操作空间，可作为部署前任务级实验的平台，具有统一的契约设计、可复现的实验流程和高保真模拟能力，为海洋滑翔机规划器的系统化评估建立了新范式。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [190] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准和HumanScore指标，用于构建感知对齐且可扩展的人形机器人运动跟踪评估体系。HumanTracker包含约153小时的光学运动轨迹数据（来自多名专业表演者），涵盖四个运动家族并带文本标签；HumanScore是在12K运动对（24K条运动）上训练、与人类偏好对齐的评估指标，能比传统运动学指标更好地捕捉接触和稳定性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪评估常与人类在视频中的直观感知不一致：运动学误差逐帧计算姿态差异，却忽略了物理伪影（如脚打滑、落地时机错误、支撑不稳定等关键问题）；同时现有测试集规模小、多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集和长时间范围的行为。

Method: 1) 构建HumanTracker基准：收集约153小时专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，按四个运动家族组织，并配备文本标签用于细粒度诊断；2) 提出HumanScore：一种偏好对齐的评估指标，在包含24K条运动的12K运动对上训练，用于预测人类偏好并检测运动和稳定性失败。

Result: 在多种代表性SOTA跟踪器上，HumanScore相比传统运动学指标能更好地预测人类偏好，并能揭示运动学指标往往遗漏的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker与HumanScore共同构成一套感知对齐且可扩展的人形跟踪评估方案，解决了传统指标的局限，为接触密集、长时程的人形运动跟踪提供更可靠的质量评估。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Matrix-Driven Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) Surfaces Structural Framework: Continuity Limitations, Computer Graphics Algorithms, and Software Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12697)
*Hakan Üstünel*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四次Overhauser（QOVR）曲面生成框架，通过可变参数的四次架构实现正交坐标轴上的精确参数隔离，达到局部形状修改与全局边界对齐的分离控制。该框架满足C^0位置闭合和C^1切向连续条件，但不维持C^2曲率连续性，局部位移效应随距离呈指数衰减，适用于制造和逆向工程的密封需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统曲面建模中边界对齐与局部形状修改之间的耦合约束问题，即需要在保持全局边界不变的前提下实现局部曲面调整，同时避免全局曲率传播、边缘接缝分离和波浪状伪影。

Method: 构建四次可变参数QOVR曲面架构，实现沿正交坐标轴的精确参数隔离。采用方向性样条混合函数与对称空间控制矩阵相结合的分析管线，使得内部节点向量变化能够触发局部曲面调整而不影响全局边界位置。

Result: 计算验证表明该框架满足C^0位置闭合和C^1切向连续性条件，但不能保持C^2曲率连续性。三维网格模型和色图可视化证实空间强度场严格限制在被修改单元的邻域内，位移效应随距扰动控制点的距离呈指数衰减，保持了结构对称性和边界不变性。

Conclusion: QOVR框架实现了局部形状控制与全局边界稳定的解耦，满足制造和逆向工程的密封标准，为CAGD领域提供了一种新的局部可控曲面生成方案。尽管不满足高阶C^2连续性，但其局部化控制特性在实际工程应用中具有明确价值。

Abstract: This study introduces the spatial and analytical construction of the Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) surface generation framework designed to resolve boundary alignment and localized shape modification constraints. This framework implements a variable parameter fourth degree novel architecture to achieve exact parameter isolation across orthogonal coordinate axes. The analytical pipeline integrates directional spline blending functions with symmetric spatial control matrices, ensuring that internal knot vector variations allow localized surface adjustments while preserving the absolute positional invariance of global edge boundaries. Computational verification confirms that while the current formulation satisfies explicit C^0 positional closure and C^1 tangent continuity conditions across the internal and boundary interfaces without triggering global curvature propagation, edge joint separation, or wave-like artifacts, C^2 curvature continuity is not maintained. As demonstrated by three-dimensional mesh models and colormap visualizations, the spatial intensity fields condense strictly within the immediate neighborhood of the modified element, verifying that the displacement effect decreases exponentially as the distance from the perturbed control points increases. This explicit decoupling preserves structural symmetry and boundary invariance across adjacent geometric patches, satisfying manufacturing and reverse engineering sealing criteria.
  Keywords: Quartic Overhauser surface; computer aided geometric design (CAGD); local shape control; geometric continuity; symmetric tensor products; computer graphics algorithms; software implementation framework.

</details>


### [192] [Blue Noise as a Lattice Gibbs Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13446)
*Zhuoran Yi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将蓝噪声生成视为吉布斯分布采样的统一框架，通过马尔可夫链反向追踪（CTTP）实现有界成本采样，并支持瓦片化生成，使得输出结果与全尺寸域生成逐位一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蓝噪声采样方法将统计建模与可扩展生成分离：优化/传输方法生成高质量点集但耦合所有样本，计算成本高；过程式/瓦片式方法局部但仅隐式定义输出。需要一种既能保证统计质量又能支持可扩展、可并行生成的统一方案。

Method: 将蓝噪声生成建模为二进制晶格占用上的吉布斯分布采样，其中密度、排斥强度、相互作用尺度和核硬度为分布参数。利用成对能量可截断的性质，将分布化简为有界度的马尔可夫随机场。采样采用Coupling Towards The Past（从期望状态反向追踪马尔可夫链并在固定深度截断）方法，从而有界化成本与区域依赖。每个瓦片独立生成并带有足够的halo，可保证与全域生成逐位一致。

Result: 模型、采样器及瓦片化保证分别得到验证：集成输出重现标准蓝噪声谱并随参数连续变化；采样器的工作量和内存符合理论预测；瓦片输出与全域生成逐位一致。在14K分辨率下演示自适应点画，内存仅取决于瓦片大小而非输出大小，并扩展到多类别的场景。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了蓝噪声采样的统计建模与可扩展生成，提供了带误差界的可控参数框架，并实现了内存与输出大小无关的瓦片化生成，为大规模自适应蓝噪声采样提供了新途径。

Abstract: Blue-noise sampling is widely used in computer graphics, but existing methods separate statistical modeling from scalable generation. Optimization and transport methods produce high-quality point sets by coupling all samples together. Procedural and tile-based samplers are local, but define their output only implicitly.
  We formulate blue-noise generation as sampling from a Gibbs distribution over binary lattice occupancies with pairwise repulsive interactions. Density, repulsion strength, interaction scale, and kernel hardness are parameters of this distribution. Because the energy sums over pairs, distant interactions can be dropped with a bounded change to the distribution, leaving a Markov random field of bounded degree.
  To sample it, we trace the Markov chain backward from the state we want, following Coupling Towards The Past, and cut the trace at a fixed depth. This bounds the cost, and it bounds the region each sample depends on. A tile generated on its own, with a sufficient halo, is then bit-identical to the same region generated on any larger domain, in any order and with no communication between tiles. Memory is set by the tile size, not by the output size, and accuracy is traded against cost through parameters with a proven error bound rather than by switching algorithms.
  We validate the model, the sampler, and these guarantees separately. The ensemble reproduces standard blue-noise spectra and moves continuously between them as its parameters vary. The sampler matches its predicted work and memory. Tiled output is verified bit-identical to full-domain generation. We demonstrate adaptive stippling at 14K, where existing methods need memory proportional to the output, along with multi-class extensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoKiFormer架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和MoE知识存储与计算耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度提升1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两个低效问题：(i)自注意力缺乏序列内部局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致冗余建模；(ii)MoE将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准decoder上新增两个模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA）：在注意力中引入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力在更具信息量的表示上运行；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM）：引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址槽位中，将存储与计算解耦，支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验表明LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。

Conclusion: 通过局部与全局信息的解耦与显式建模，LoKiFormer在效率和效果上均优于现有LLM架构，验证了其架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [194] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气数据），针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区三种冰川相关灾害（冰碛坝/冰坝溃决洪水、降雨触发的滑坡、冰川上及周边小池塘洪水）分别建立"易感性"（哪里）与"触发条件"（何时）预测模型。在严格空间交叉验证下，天气触发模型ROC分别达0.73/0.83/0.82，而基于地形的易感性排名在匹配可比邻近地点后诚实数值为0.76/0.71/0.54（小洪水接近随机）。深度学习模型未显著超越梯度提升基线，大溃决的触发信号在尼泊尔单一区域内可达ROC 0.89。文章以尼泊尔风险预警清单作结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前可行性研究仅证明变形指示湖泊失稳、天气指示风险时机，但未提出预测模型。作者旨在填补这一空白，仅用免费数据构建并评估"哪个地点易感、何时触发"的预测模型，为缺乏昂贵监测手段的喜马拉雅冰川湖溃决风险提供可操作的预警工具。

Method: 使用589条HMAGLOFDB记录的冰湖溃决事件及数千条编目滑坡事件，每条事件与类似但未失稳的地点配对。采用空间交叉验证（隐藏整块地图瓦片）防止邻域泄漏，强制所有模型对照强简单基线。模型测试三组灾害，每组回答两个独立问题（易感性与触发时机）。天气触发模型基于前置天气条件，地形易感性模型结合匹配后的稳健评估，并测试了五种深度学习模型与梯度提升基线的对比，以及三规则决策树的可解释性检验。

Result: 前置天气定时的ROC：大溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形易感性朴素评分约0.9但主要因编目灾害聚集在湿润区域；匹配可比邻近地点后为0.76、0.71和0.54（无区分度）。大溃决信号在单一尼泊尔区域内达0.89。五种深度学习模型未决定性超越梯度提升基线，仅滑坡上三项略高但不显著；湖泊灾害中基线全胜，并由"崎岖度+季风降雨"三规则决策树复现。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以提供有意义的灾害定时预测（尤其大溃决在尼泊尔境内信号强），但地形易感性预测仅在部分灾害类型上有效，小洪水场景下地形几乎无预测力。深度学习在此任务上不带来决定性增益，简单基线更稳健。最终产出尼泊尔风险优先排序清单作为决策辅助而非确定性预测，并明确指出免费数据的能力边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [195] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态的检查点作为"状态锚点"并为其生成内容条件化的锚键，使状态空间模型能够在保持线性计算效率的同时扩展记忆容量，从而在长上下文检索任务上超越多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer依赖随上下文长度增长的token级记忆，但训练时具有二次方计算复杂度，推理时KV缓存线性增长；而循环替代方案虽然解码高效，但将所有历史压缩为固定大小的状态，导致早期的关联信息被后续更新覆盖，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。

Method: MARCH周期性地将累积的循环状态检查点缓存为状态锚点，并为每个锚点生成紧凑的内容条件化锚键，从而维护一个随上下文长度增长的记忆库。在每个token处，MARCH生成锚点查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力聚合计算输出。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文内检索任务上一致性地优于多种线性注意力变体。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存能够显著增强循环模型的远程记忆能力，同时保留其原生计算路径的高效性，为状态空间模型提供了超越固定维度限制的可扩展记忆方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构），用于多AUV自组网中对机动目标进行协同跟踪。该方法将水下任务建模为MDP，通过三层闭环控制框架和值梯度引导的扩散策略，在受限声学通信和动态拓扑条件下实现更快的收敛、更高的跟踪精度和更稳定的训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网目标跟踪面临水下声学通信受限、拓扑动态变化和海洋扰动不确定等挑战。现有基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模和策略生成对噪声敏感的问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化，亟需更稳健的策略学习算法。

Method: 提出了两个核心组件：（1）VGG-MADiffRL，一种值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法，在逆向去噪过程中引入值梯度来引导动作生成朝向更高期望回报，并采用孪生价值网络、联合优化和软目标更新来缓解过估计和训练振荡；（2）MDCA，一种基于扩散的分层控制架构，由全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层和物理动作执行层构成三层闭环控制框架，实现任务分配、局部决策和执行反馈的协同优化。同时将声纳探测机制和洋流扰动建模为MDP。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和实际工程价值。

Conclusion: 值梯度引导的扩散策略为水下多AUV协同跟踪提供了稳定的策略学习方法，MDCA三层架构有效解耦了任务分配、决策与执行，该方法在动态水下环境中表现出良好的收敛性与跟踪性能，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [197] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式建模统一表述为一个路径积分框架，其中流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型均是该统一作用量的不同评估原则实现。基于MSRJD形式将该框架分为自由和相互作用概率流，从而引入图论微扰理论，并导出一圈修正项可在无随机采样成本下改善确定性采样器，同时为学习分数的不完美性提供了响应加权得分匹配目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式建模方法（流、扩散、变分、对抗）虽然在实践上各有成功，但缺乏一个统一的、具有理论深度的框架来理解它们之间的关系，也缺乏系统性的方法对确定性采样器进行更高阶修正，以及量化学习分数不完美带来的影响。

Method: 将生成式建模表述为路径积分（作用量），利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de-Dominicis (MSRJD)形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而将生成模型纳入图论微扰理论框架。该方法支持一圈修正、将不完美学习分数作为插入项处理，以及通过对称性等变漂移设计的算符展开（EFT阚度功率计数）来系统化模型构建。

Result: 在可解和非线性漂移上验证了一圈修正方法，将53%的树级误差降至1.6%；推导出响应加权得分匹配目标用于处理不完美的学习分数；建立了对称性等变漂移设计的算符展开框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成式建模提供了一个统一的路径积分理论框架，弥合了多种现有方法的鸿沟，并通过微扰论实现了计算高效的采样器改进，为后续理论驱动的生成模型设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [198] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，一种面向动态图边级异常检测模型AddGraph的严格后验可解释性框架，基于双空间-时间归因（DSTA）机制，在不改变检测性能（ΔAUC=0）的前提下为异常边提供可审计的空间和时间解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习异常检测器虽然准确率很高，但其不透明性使得在合作监管的信息系统中难以满足审计和可信性要求。AddGraph作为动态图边级异常检测的基础框架，此前从未配备任何形式的可解释机制，这一空白亟需填补。

Method: 提出后验可解释框架X-AddGraph，基于双空间-时间归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块对齐的组件：（1）基于梯度的当前邻接结构相关归因（空间）；(2)直接读取推断中已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间，零额外成本）；（3）通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间）。检测器保持冻结状态。

Result: 检测性能完全保留（ΔAUC=0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果。在四种边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）评估中，长期归因识别出历史快照携带显著更多的反事实信号（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲可解释器无法提供的。实现已开源。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph在不牺牲检测性能的前提下为AddGraph提供了多层可解释性，其中长期时间归因能挖掘历史快照中的反事实信号，为动态图异常检测的可审计性和可信性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [199] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期参考图（LBH）构建方法，利用多评分者数据集中的混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的分期行为，聚合多个专家的概率来生成更可靠的参考分期标签。在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上，使用EEG+EMG特征和随机森林分类器获得最佳性能，准确率约86%，相较于数据集分期图（DH）和最佳评分者分期图（BSH）均有一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期对睡眠障碍的诊断和管理至关重要，但大多数自动分期研究仅与单一参考分期图对比，忽视了已知的评分者间变异性。现有方法未能充分利用多评分者数据集中多位专家的集体智慧来构建更可靠的参考标签。

Method: 提出学习型分期图（LBH）方法：使用EEG（C3-M2）和颏肌EMG信号，按30秒时段分割并提取每种模态30个特征（共60个特征）。核心思路是利用机器学习模型（随机森林、支持向量机、多层感知机）推导每位评分者的混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估算各真实睡眠阶段的概率，再跨评分者聚合概率以分配每个时段的最终标签。

Result: LBH在所有分类器和特征设置下均一致提升了整体性能。最佳结果为随机森林结合EEG+EMG设置：DOD-H上准确率86.07%、精确率85.46%、F1-score 85.29%；DOD-O上准确率86.04%、精确率85.21%、F1-score 84.70%。优于数据集分期图（DH）和最佳评分者分期图（BSH）。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模可以在不丢弃单个专家信息的前提下改进参考分期图的构建质量，为多评分者数据集的利用提供了新思路，有望提升睡眠自动分期的评估可靠性。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [200] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer残差流中存在以"预测方向"（unembedding方向）为内容定义的特权锚点。以该锚点测量时，残差流的几何和行为结构与预测距离呈分层关系。该分层在全部18种模型（密集/MoE、7B-120B、基础/指令微调）中成立，且预测方向与主方差轴近乎正交，导致基于方差的分析只能部分捕获该组织。预测近端区域高度结构化且因果决定性，远端区域平坦但承载负荷，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已训练Transformer会发展出特权基（坐标轴统计特性与残差流其余部分不同），但此前不清楚这种基选择的是什么方向。作者希望探究预测方向（当前预测token的unembedding方向）是否及如何作为一种内容定义的特权锚点，从而解释高维计算如何与线性读出共存这一根本性问题。

Method: 以预测方向作为锚点对残差流进行几何分析：1）测量残差流变异性相对预测方向的分层结构；2）对比方差分析（PCA/主方差轴）与锚定分析的结果差异；3）通过因果扰动实验（破坏最接近预测方向/次近/远端方差方向）检验各区域的功能影响；4）在18种不同规模、架构和训练方式的模型上进行系统性验证。

Result: 预测方向作为特权锚点成立：它构建了一个窄而尺度不变的"预测接口"集中读出相关结构，而预测远端的大片区域随模型规模扩张。预测方向与主方差轴近乎正交，方差分析仅能部分恢复该组织且缺口随提示异质性增大。近端区域高度结构化、聚类相关提示，远端区域更平坦并反判别提示组。因果实验显示：破坏最接近预测方向的方向导致立即分歧和任务框架转换，破坏次近方向延迟分歧但保留框架，远端方向每方向弱对齐但因果和时间上承载负荷。行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一种不同于以往描述坐标轴的特权锚点。该发现提供了一种几何机制来解释高维Transformer计算如何与线性读出共存：窄的预测接口负责读出，而广阔的远端空间承载计算，二者通过预测方向锚定有机整合。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [201] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，用于对多个飞行姿态下突发阵风引起的压力载荷数据进行分类，该方法通过表征学习和摘要选择最小显著样本集，在3480个实验测量中识别出9种在多个姿态间反复出现的基础响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对阵风引起的载荷进行分类依赖于粗略参数（如飞行姿态）进行标签化，缺乏客观的分类准则。作者希望找到一种既客观（不依赖先验粗参数）又可解释（保持类似粗参数标签的可理解性）的分类方法，以组织跨多个飞行条件下的阵风载荷复杂性。

Method: 该方法首先通过机器学习表征将大量实验观测编码为向量表示，然后应用摘要（summarization）程序选择最小的高显著性样本子集（exemplars）。这些exemplars提供了基于相似性的客观分类准则，便于专家检查和进一步实验。

Result: 在包含3480个阵风压力载荷测量数据（来自六个飞行姿态的飞翼模型）的数据库上，该方法识别出9种在多个飞行姿态间反复出现的基础响应类型。对每种类型的瞬态响应分析揭示了其背后的流体力学物理直觉。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于机器学习的表征与摘要方法可以提供客观且可解释的阵风载荷分类准则，发现的基础响应类型跨越多个飞行姿态，既有助于专家理解潜在的流体力学机理，也为更精细的实验设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions?
